   16: =head1 NAME
   17: 
   18: HTML::D3 - A simple Perl module for generating charts using D3.js.
   19: 
   20: =head1 VERSION
   21: 
   22: Version 0.23
   23: 
   24: =cut
   25: 
   26: our $VERSION = '0.23';
   27: 
   28: =head1 SYNOPSIS
   29: 
   30:     use HTML::D3;
   31: 
   32:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(
   33:         width => 1024,
   34:         height => 768,
   35:         title => 'Sample Bar Chart'
   36:     );
   37: 
   38:     my $data = [
   39:         ['Category 1', 10],
   40:         ['Category 2', 20],
   41:         ['Category 3', 30]
   42:     ];
   43: 
   44:     my $html = $chart->render_bar_chart($data);
   45:     print $html;
   46: 
   47:     $chart = HTML::D3->new(title => 'Sales Data');
   48: 
   49:     $data = [
   50:         ['Product A', 100],
   51:         ['Product B', 150],
   52:         ['Product C', 200]
   53:     ];
   54: 
   55:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart($data);
   56:     print $html;
   57: 
   58: =head1 DESCRIPTION
   59: 
   60: HTML::D3 is a Perl module that provides functionality to create simple charts using D3.js.
   61: The module generates HTML and JavaScript code to render the chart in a web browser.
   62: 
   63: =head1 METHODS
   64: 
   65: The C<=head3 API SPECIFICATION> subsections use L<Params::Validate::Strict>
   66: schema syntax (C<< type => 'arrayref' >> etc.) as a documentation convention.
   67: The module is also used at runtime in C<new()> to validate constructor
   68: arguments; it is therefore a required runtime dependency.  The schemas describe
   69: the parameter contract in machine-readable notation and can be plumbed into a
   70: WAF or test generator if desired.
   71: 
   72: =head2 new
   73: 
   74:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(%args);
   75: 
   76: Creates a new HTML::D3 object.
   77: Accepts the following optional arguments:
   78: 
   79: =over 4
   80: 
   81: =item * C<width> - The width of the chart (default: 800).
   82: 
   83: =item * C<height> - The height of the chart (default: 600).
   84: 
   85: =item * C<title> - The title of the chart (default: 'Chart').
   86: 
   87: =item * C<responsive> - If true, SVG elements are rendered with
   88: C<viewBox> and C<width="100%" height="auto"> instead of fixed pixel
   89: dimensions, so the chart scales fluidly with its container.
   90: For snippet methods the per-call C<< opts => { responsive => 1 } >>
   91: takes precedence over this object-level setting (default: 0).
   92: 
   93: =back
   94: 
   95: =cut
   96:

  144: # â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  145: # The categorical colour schemes, written down once.
  146: #
  147: # The scheme names and the colours themselves live here and nowhere else.  The
  148: # snippets inline these colours as a JavaScript array - not as a d3.scheme*
  149: # expression - so a page draws the exact numbers palette() hands a map, and a
  150: # change to d3's own palette cannot move a chart's colours under it.  Perl
  151: # callers - a map, a legend, a static renderer - read the same colours through
  152: # categorical_schemes(), palette() and colour_at().  A chart and a map of the
  153: # same data therefore cannot disagree about what scheme 'set2' means, which two
  154: # separate lists of names and colours eventually would.
  155: #
  156: # The colours are D3's own, in D3's order: the scheme arrays in
  157: # d3-scale-chromatic 3.x, which is the version d3 v7 - what _head() loads -
  158: # bundles.  Each entry names the d3 scheme it transcribes, so the palette can be
  159: # checked against the library (t/d3-schemes.t does that when Node and d3 are
  160: # installed), but the chart does not depend on d3 for the numbers it draws.
  161: my %CATEGORICAL_SCHEMES = (
  162: 	tableau10 => {
  163: 		colours => [ '#4e79a7', '#f28e2c', '#e15759', '#76b7b2', '#59a14f',
  164: 			'#edc949', '#af7aa1', '#ff9da7', '#9c755f', '#bab0ab' ],
  165: 	}, category10 => {
  166: 		colours => [ '#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e', '#2ca02c', '#d62728', '#9467bd',
  167: 			'#8c564b', '#e377c2', '#7f7f7f', '#bcbd22', '#17becf' ],
  168: 	}, set2 => {
  169: 		colours => [ '#66c2a5', '#fc8d62', '#8da0cb', '#e78ac3',
  170: 			'#a6d854', '#ffd92f', '#e5c494', '#b3b3b3' ],
  171: 	}, set3 => {
  172: 		colours => [ '#8dd3c7', '#ffffb3', '#bebada', '#fb8072', '#80b1d3',
  173: 			'#fdb462', '#b3de69', '#fccde5', '#d9d9d9', '#bc80bd',
  174: 			'#ccebc5', '#ffed6f' ],
  175: 	}, paired => {
  176: 		colours => [ '#a6cee3', '#1f78b4', '#b2df8a', '#33a02c', '#fb9a99',
  177: 			'#e31a1c', '#fdbf6f', '#ff7f00', '#cab2d6', '#6a3d9a',
  178: 			'#ffff99', '#b15928' ],
  179: 	},
  180: );
  181: 
  182: # The scheme a chart draws with when it is not told which one to use.
  183: my $DEFAULT_CATEGORICAL_SCHEME = 'tableau10';
  184: 
  185: # The palette the charts that offer no scheme option draw with: the pie family
  186: # and the multi-series line charts that predate the option have always drawn
  187: # category10.  The colours are read from the definition above rather than
  188: # written out again here, so that a chart with no choice and a chart told which
  189: # scheme to use are the same palette and not two copies of it.
  190: my $CATEGORY10_JS = _scheme_colours_js('category10');
  191: 
  192: =head2 categorical_schemes
  193: 
  194:     my $names = HTML::D3->categorical_schemes();
  195: 
  196: Returns an arrayref of the names of the categorical colour schemes this module
  197: can draw with, sorted alphabetically: C<category10>, C<paired>, C<set2>,
  198: C<set3> and C<tableau10>.
  199: These are the names the C<scheme> and C<color_scheme> options accept.
  200: 
  201: =cut
  202: 
  203: sub categorical_schemes
  204: {
  205: 	return [ sort keys %CATEGORICAL_SCHEMES ];
  206: }
  207: 
  208: =head2 palette
  209: 
  210:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette('set2');
  211: 
  212: Returns an arrayref of the colours of one scheme - the same colours, in the same
  213: order, that a chart drawn with C<< scheme => 'set2' >> draws with.
  214: A map, a legend or a static renderer can therefore colour the same data the way
  215: the chart does, from Perl, without a browser:
  216: 
  217:     my $scheme  = 'set2';
  218:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette($scheme);
  219:     my $index   = 0;
  220:     for my $category (@categories) {
  221:         printf "%s is %s\n", $category, HTML::D3->colour_at($scheme, $index++);
  222:     }
  223: 
  224: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for a name that is not in
  225: C<categorical_schemes()>, rather than quietly returning the default scheme's
  226: colours, so that a misspelt scheme is found where it is asked for.
  227: The arrayref returned is a copy: pushing onto it does not change the palette.
  228: 
  229: =head2 colour_at
  230: 
  231:     my $fill = HTML::D3->colour_at('tableau10', $index);
  232: 
  233: Returns the colour one scheme gives the C<$index>th category, counting from
  234: zero, cyclically: an index past the end of the palette wraps around to its
  235: start, which is how C<d3.scaleOrdinal> - what the snippets draw with - assigns
  236: colours.
  237: Colour 0 is the first colour of the scheme, the one C<render_histogram_snippet>
  238: uses as the bar fill when it is given a C<scheme>.
  239: 
  240: The British spelling matches the C<colour_by_series> option.
  241: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for an unknown scheme, and with
  242: C<Colour index must be a non-negative integer> for an index that is not one.
  243: 
  244: =cut
  245: 
  246: sub palette
  247: {
  248: 	my ($self, $scheme) = @_;
  249: 
  250: 	return [ @{_categorical_scheme($scheme)->{colours}} ];
  251: }
  252: 
  253: sub colour_at
  254: {
  255: 	my ($self, $scheme, $index) = @_;
  256: 
  257: 	die 'Colour index must be a non-negative integer'
  258: 		unless defined($index) && looks_like_number($index)
  259: 			&& $index == int($index) && $index >= 0;
  260: 
  261: 	my $colours = _categorical_scheme($scheme)->{colours};
  262: 
  263: 	return $colours->[$index % scalar(@$colours)];
  264: }

  312: # Method to render a bar chart with given data
  313: sub render_bar_chart {
  314: 	my ($self, $data) = @_;
  315: 
  316: 	die 'Data is not optional' if(!defined($data));
  317: 
  318: 	# Validate input data to ensure it is an array of arrays
  319: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($data) eq 'ARRAY';
  320: 
  321: 	# Generate JSON representation of data
  322: 	my $json_data = $_JSON->encode([
  323: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
  324: 	]);
  325: 
  326: 	# Generate HTML and D3.js JavaScript for rendering the bar chart
  327: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
  328: 	$html .= $self->_head();
  329: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  330: <body>
  331:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
  332:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  333:     <script>
  334: 	const data = $json_data;
  335: 
  336: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  337: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
  338: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
  339: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  340: 
  341: 	// Set up scales for x and y axes
  342: 	const x = d3.scaleBand()
  343: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
  344: 	    .range([0, width])
  345: 	    .padding(0.1);
  346: 
  347: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
  348: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
  349: 	    .nice()
  350: 	    .range([height, 0]);
  351: 
  352: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
  353: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
  354: 
  355: 	// Add bars to the chart
  356: 	chart.append("g")
  357: 	    .selectAll("rect")
  358: 	    .data(data)
  359: 	    .join("rect")
  360: 	    .attr("x", d => x(d.label))
  361: 	    .attr("y", d => y(d.value))
  362: 	    .attr("height", d => height - y(d.value))
  363: 	    .attr("width", x.bandwidth())
  364: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue");
  365: 
  366: 	// Add the y-axis
  367: 	chart.append("g")
  368: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
  369: 
  370: 	// Add the x-axis with labels rotated for better readability
  371: 	chart.append("g")
  372: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
  373: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
  374: 	    .selectAll("text")
  375: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
  376: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
  377:     </script>
  378: </body>
  379: </html>
  380: HTML
  381: 
  382:     return $html;
  383: }

  388: 
  389: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a bar chart where each bar grows

  431: sub render_animated_bar_chart {
  432: 	my ($self, $data) = @_;
  433: 
  434: 	die 'Data is not optional' if(!defined($data));
  435: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($data) eq 'ARRAY';
  436: 
  437: 	my $json_data = $_JSON->encode([
  438: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
  439: 	]);
  440: 
  441: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
  442: 	$html .= $self->_head();
  443: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  444: <body>
  445:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
  446:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  447:     <script>
  448: 	const data = $json_data;
  449: 
  450: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  451: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
  452: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
  453: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  454: 
  455: 	const x = d3.scaleBand()
  456: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
  457: 	    .range([0, width])
  458: 	    .padding(0.1);
  459: 
  460: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
  461: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
  462: 	    .nice()
  463: 	    .range([height, 0]);
  464: 
  465: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
  466: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
  467: 
  468: 	// Each bar starts at the baseline (y=height, height=0) and grows upward.
  469: 	chart.append("g")
  470: 	    .selectAll("rect")
  471: 	    .data(data)
  472: 	    .join("rect")
  473: 	    .attr("x", d => x(d.label))
  474: 	    .attr("y", height)
  475: 	    .attr("height", 0)
  476: 	    .attr("width", x.bandwidth())
  477: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue")
  478: 	    .transition()
  479: 	    .duration(800)
  480: 	    .delay((d, i) => i * 100)
  481: 	    .attr("y", d => y(d.value))
  482: 	    .attr("height", d => height - y(d.value));
  483: 
  484: 	chart.append("g")
  485: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
  486: 
  487: 	chart.append("g")
  488: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
  489: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
  490: 	    .selectAll("text")
  491: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
  492: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
  493:     </script>
  494: </body>
  495: </html>
  496: HTML
  497: 
  498: 	return $html;
  499: }

  555: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  556: <body>
  557:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
  558:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  559:     <script>
  560: 	const data = $json_data;
  561: 
  562: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  563: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
  564: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
  565: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  566: 
  567: 	const x = d3.scalePoint()
  568: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
  569: 	    .range([0, width]);
  570: 
  571: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
  572: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
  573: 	    .nice()
  574: 	    .range([height, 0]);
  575: 
  576: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
  577: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
  578: 
  579: 	// Draw line
  580: 	const line = d3.line()
  581: 	    .x(d => x(d.label))
  582: 	    .y(d => y(d.value));
  583: 
  584: 	chart.append("path")
  585: 	    .datum(data)
  586: 	    .attr("fill", "none")
  587: 	    .attr("stroke", "steelblue")
  588: 	    .attr("stroke-width", 2)
  589: 	    .attr("d", line);
  590: 
  591: 	// Add points to the line
  592: 	chart.selectAll("circle")
  593: 	    .data(data)
  594: 	    .join("circle")
  595: 	    .attr("cx", d => x(d.label))
  596: 	    .attr("cy", d => y(d.value))
  597: 	    .attr("r", 4)
  598: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue");
  599: 
  600: 	// Add axes
  601: 	chart.append("g")
  602: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
  603: 
  604: 	chart.append("g")
  605: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
  606: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
  607: 	    .selectAll("text")
  608: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
  609: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
  610:     </script>
  611: </body>
  612: </html>
  613: HTML
  614: 
  615: 	return $html;
  616: }

  618: =head2 render_animated_line_chart
  619: 
  620:     my $html = $chart->render_animated_line_chart($data);

  Mutants (Total: 2, Killed: 2, Survived: 0)
  621: 
  622: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a line chart where the line
  623: draws itself from left to right on page load, followed by each data-point
  624: circle fading in once the line is complete.
  625: Accepts the following arguments:
  626: 
  627: =over 4
  628: 
  629: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each data point is an
  630: array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
  631: 
  632: =back
  633: 
  634: Returns a string containing the complete HTML5 document.
  635: 
  636: =head3 Errors
  637: 
  638: =over 4
  639: 
  640: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
  641:

  643: 
  644: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
  645: 
  646: =head4 Input
  647: 
  648:     {
  649:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
  650:     }
  651: 
  652:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
  653:     non-arrayref dies.
  654: 
  655: =head4 Output
  656: 
  657:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; the line path animates via
  658:            C<stroke-dashoffset> with C<d3.easeLinear>; data-point circles
  659:            fade in with C<opacity> after the line transition completes.
  660: 
  661: =cut
  662: 
  663: sub render_animated_line_chart {
  664: 	my ($self, $data) = @_;
  665: 
  666: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($data) eq 'ARRAY';
  667: 
  668: 	my $json_data = $_JSON->encode([
  669: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
  670: 	]);
  671: 
  672: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
  673: 	$html .= $self->_head();
  674: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  675: <body>
  676:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
  677:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  678:     <script>
  679: 	const data = $json_data;
  680: 
  681: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  682: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
  683: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
  684: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  685: 
  686: 	const x = d3.scalePoint()
  687: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
  688: 	    .range([0, width]);
  689: 
  690: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
  691: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
  692: 	    .nice()
  693: 	    .range([height, 0]);
  694: 
  695: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
  696: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
  697: 
  698: 	const line = d3.line()
  699: 	    .x(d => x(d.label))
  700: 	    .y(d => y(d.value));
  701: 
  702: 	// Animate the line drawing left-to-right using stroke-dashoffset.
  703: 	const path = chart.append("path")
  704: 	    .datum(data)
  705: 	    .attr("fill", "none")
  706: 	    .attr("stroke", "steelblue")
  707: 	    .attr("stroke-width", 2)
  708: 	    .attr("d", line);
  709: 
  710: 	const totalLength = path.node().getTotalLength();
  711: 
  712: 	path
  713: 	    .attr("stroke-dasharray", totalLength)
  714: 	    .attr("stroke-dashoffset", totalLength)
  715: 	    .transition()
  716: 	    .duration(1500)
  717: 	    .ease(d3.easeLinear)
  718: 	    .attr("stroke-dashoffset", 0);
  719: 
  720: 	// Data-point circles fade in after the line finishes.
  721: 	chart.selectAll("circle")
  722: 	    .data(data)
  723: 	    .join("circle")
  724: 	    .attr("cx", d => x(d.label))
  725: 	    .attr("cy", d => y(d.value))
  726: 	    .attr("r", 4)
  727: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue")
  728: 	    .attr("opacity", 0)
  729: 	    .transition()
  730: 	    .delay(1500)
  731: 	    .duration(300)
  732: 	    .attr("opacity", 1);
  733: 
  734: 	chart.append("g")
  735: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
  736: 
  737: 	chart.append("g")
  738: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
  739: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
  740: 	    .selectAll("text")
  741: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
  742: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
  743:     </script>
  744: </body>
  745: </html>
  746: HTML
  747: 
  748: 	return $html;
  749: }

  751: =head2 render_pie_chart
  752: 
  753:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data);

  Mutants (Total: 2, Killed: 2, Survived: 0)
  754:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data, { separator => ':' });
  755: 
  756: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a pie chart.
  757: Each slice is coloured with the ten hex values of C<d3.schemeCategory10>,
  758: inlined into the page rather than read from D3's own palette (see L</palette>);
  759: percentage labels appear inside each slice and a colour legend is shown to the
  760: right of the pie.
  761: Accepts the following arguments:
  762: 
  763: =over 4
  764: 
  765: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each data point is an
  766: array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
  767: 
  768: =item * C<%opts> - Optional hashref of options.
  769: 
  770: =over 8
  771: 
  772: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
  773: label and value in the SVG legend.
  774: 
  775: =back
  776: 
  777: =back
  778: 
  779: Returns a string containing the complete HTML5 document.
  780: 
  781: =head3 Errors
  782: 
  783: =over 4
  784: 
  785: =item * Throws C<Data is not optional> when C<$data> is C<undef>.
  786: 
  787: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
  788: 
  789: =back
  790: 
  791: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
  792: 
  793: =head4 Input
  794: 
  795:     {
  796:         data => {
  797: 		type => 'arrayref',
  798: 		element_type => [ 'string', 'number' ]
  799: 	},
  800:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
  801:     }
  802: 
  803:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
  804:     non-arrayref dies.
  805:     Recognised C<opts> key: C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>)
  806:     - character shown between label and value in the SVG legend.
  807: 
  808: =head4 Output
  809: 
  810:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; pie rendered with C<d3.pie()> and
  811:            C<d3.arc()>; slices coloured with category10's hex values,
  812:            inlined; percentage label inside each slice; legend to the right.
  813: 
  814: =cut
  815: 
  816: sub render_pie_chart {
  817: 	my ($self, $data, $opts) = @_;
  818: 	$opts //= {};
  819: 
  820: 	die 'Data is not optional' if(!defined($data));
  821: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($data) eq 'ARRAY';
  822: 
  823: 	my $separator = $opts->{separator} // '/';
  824: 
  825: 	my $json_data = $_JSON->encode([
  826: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
  827: 	]);
  828: 
  829: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
  830: 	$html .= $self->_head();
  831: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  832: <body>
  833:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
  834:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  835:     <script>
  836: 	const data = $json_data;
  837: 
  838: 	const width = $self->{width};
  839: 	const height = $self->{height};
  840: 	const radius = Math.min(width, height) / 2 - 40;
  841: 
  842: 	const color = d3.scaleOrdinal($CATEGORY10_JS);
  843: 
  844: 	const pie = d3.pie()
  845: 	    .sort(null)
  846: 	    .value(d => d.value);
  847: 
  848: 	const arc = d3.arc()
  849: 	    .innerRadius(0)
  850: 	    .outerRadius(radius);
  851: 
  852: 	const labelArc = d3.arc()
  853: 	    .innerRadius(radius * 0.65)
  854: 	    .outerRadius(radius * 0.65);
  855: 
  856: 	const total = d3.sum(data, d => d.value);
  857: 
  858: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  859: 
  860: 	const pieGroup = svg.append("g")
  861: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${width * 0.45},\${height / 2})`);
  862: 
  863: 	const arcs = pieGroup.selectAll(".arc")
  864: 	    .data(pie(data))
  865: 	    .join("g")
  866: 	    .attr("class", "arc");
  867: 
  868: 	arcs.append("path")
  869: 	    .attr("d", arc)
  870: 	    .attr("fill", d => color(d.data.label))
  871: 	    .attr("stroke", "white")
  872: 	    .style("stroke-width", "2px");
  873: 
  874: 	arcs.append("text")
  875: 	    .attr("transform", d => `translate(\${labelArc.centroid(d)})`)
  876: 	    .attr("text-anchor", "middle")
  877: 	    .attr("font-size", "11px")
  878: 	    .attr("fill", "white")
  879: 	    .attr("pointer-events", "none")
  880: 	    .text(d => Math.round(d.data.value / total * 100) + "%");
  881: 
  882: 	// Legend
  883: 	const legend = svg.append("g")
  884: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${width * 0.72},\${(height - data.length * 22) / 2})`);
  885: 
  886: 	legend.selectAll("rect")
  887: 	    .data(pie(data))
  888: 	    .join("rect")
  889: 	    .attr("x", 0)
  890: 	    .attr("y", (d, i) => i * 22)
  891: 	    .attr("width", 14)
  892: 	    .attr("height", 14)
  893: 	    .attr("fill", d => color(d.data.label));
  894: 
  895: 	legend.selectAll("text")
  896: 	    .data(pie(data))
  897: 	    .join("text")
  898: 	    .attr("x", 20)
  899: 	    .attr("y", (d, i) => i * 22 + 11)
  900: 	    .attr("font-size", "12px")
  901: 	    .text(d => `\${d.data.label} $separator \${d.data.value}`);
  902:     </script>
  903: </body>
  904: </html>
  905: HTML
  906: 
  907: 	return $html;
  908: }

 1078: =head2 render_pie_chart_snippet

  Mutants (Total: 2, Killed: 2, Survived: 0)
 1079: 
 1080:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices);
 1081:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices, \%opts);
 1082:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'pie_chart'
 1083:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1084: 
 1085: Generates an embeddable pie or donut chart fragment for use in existing HTML
 1086: layouts.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'pie_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1087: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1088: 
 1089: =head3 Data format
 1090: 
 1091: Each element of C<\@slices> is C<[$label, $value]> or C<[$label, $value, \%extra]>.
 1092: Negative values are silently converted to their absolute value.  Zero-value
 1093: slices are silently omitted.  C<\%extra> key/value pairs are shown as
 1094: additional rows in the hover tooltip.
 1095: 
 1096: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1097: 
 1098: =over 4
 1099: 
 1100: =item * C<animated> (bool, default 0) - fan slices in from arc-length 0 on
 1101: first render using C<attrTween> / C<d3.easeBackOut> (800 ms, staggered).
 1102: Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 1103: 
 1104: =item * C<donut> (bool, default 0) - render as a donut chart (inner radius
 1105: 38% of outer radius); the total sum appears in the centre hole.
 1106: 
 1107: =item * C<sort_slices> (string, default C<'none'>) - C<'value'> for
 1108: largest-first, C<'label'> for alphabetical, C<'none'> for input order.
 1109: 
 1110: =item * C<max_slices> (int, default 0) - when E<gt> 0, only the top N-1
 1111: slices are shown individually; the rest are collapsed into an C<"Other"> slice.
 1112: 
 1113: =item * C<legend> (bool, default 1) - render an HTML legend panel beside the chart.
 1114: 
 1115: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical
 1116: colour scheme.  Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>,
 1117: C<paired> - the names C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()>
 1118: makes available to Perl.
 1119: 
 1120: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
 1121: label and value in each legend entry (e.g. C<'/'> produces
 1122: C<Label / 12.34 (42.0%)>, C<':'> produces C<Label : 12.34 (42.0%)>).
 1123: 
 1124: =back
 1125: 
 1126: =head3 Errors
 1127:

 1398: =head2 render_heatmap_snippet
 1399: 
 1400:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples);
 1401:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples, \%opts);
 1402:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'heatmap'
 1403:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1404: 
 1405: Generates an embeddable grid heatmap for use in existing HTML layouts.
 1406: Each cell sits at the intersection of an X-axis label and a Y-axis label;
 1407: its colour encodes the cell's numeric value using a sequential D3 colour
 1408: scale.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'heatmap', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1409: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1410: 
 1411: =head3 Data format
 1412: 
 1413: Each element of C<\@triples> is C<[$x_label, $y_label, $value]>.
 1414: C<$value> must be numeric or C<undef> (C<undef> rows are silently
 1415: skipped).  Zero is a valid value and maps to the lightest cell colour.
 1416: The caller is responsible for any aggregation: if multiple triples share
 1417: the same (x_label, y_label) pair, the last one wins.
 1418: 
 1419: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1420: 
 1421: =over 4
 1422: 
 1423: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'YlOrRd'>) - D3 sequential
 1424: colour scheme.  Supported: C<YlOrRd>, C<Blues>, C<Greens>, C<Purples>,
 1425: C<RdPu>, C<YlGnBu>.
 1426: 
 1427: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title below the X axis.
 1428: The value is JavaScript-escaped (backslash, double-quote, newline, carriage
 1429: return) before being embedded in the page; other characters are taken
 1430: literally.
 1431: 
 1432: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title left of the Y axis.
 1433: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1434: 
 1435: =item * C<val_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Tooltip value label.
 1436: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1437: 
 1438: =item * C<show_values> (bool, default 0) - Print value inside each cell.
 1439: Auto-suppressed when any cell is narrower than 28 px.
 1440: 
 1441: =item * C<cell_padding> (int 0-8, default 2) - Gap in pixels between cells.
 1442: 
 1443: =item * C<legend> (bool, default 1) - Render a colour-scale legend bar.
 1444: 
 1445: =item * C<animated> (bool, default 0) - Fade cells in on first load.
 1446: Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 1447: 
 1448: =back
 1449: 
 1450: =head3 Errors
 1451: 
 1452: =over 4
 1453: 
 1454: =item * Dies with C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<\@triples>
 1455: is not an ARRAY reference.
 1456: 
 1457: =item * Dies with C<Each data point must be an array reference> when a
 1458: triple element is not an arrayref.
 1459: 
 1460: =item * Dies with C<Each data point must have at least 3 elements> when
 1461: a triple has fewer than 3 elements.
 1462: 
 1463: =item * Dies with C<Value must be numeric> when C<$value> is defined but
 1464: not numeric.
 1465: 
 1466: =item * Dies with C<Unknown color_scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for an
 1467: unsupported C<color_scheme> value.
 1468: 
 1469: =item * Dies with C<cell_padding must be between 0 and 8> when
 1470: C<cell_padding> is outside the valid range.
 1471: 
 1472: =back
 1473: 
 1474: =head3 Side Effects
 1475: 
 1476: Appends a tooltip C<div> to the page when the fragment is rendered in
 1477: the browser.
 1478: 
 1479: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1480: 
 1481: =head4 Input
 1482: 
 1483:     {
 1484:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 1485:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
 1486:     }
 1487: 
 1488:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Str, Num|undef ]>.
 1489:     Recognised C<opts> keys: C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'YlOrRd'>),
 1490:     C<x_label> (string, default C<''>), C<y_label> (string, default C<''>),
 1491:     C<val_label> (string, default C<'Value'>), C<show_values> (boolean,
 1492:     default C<0>), C<cell_padding> (integer 0-8, default C<2>),
 1493:     C<legend> (boolean, default C<1>), C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>).
 1494: 
 1495: =head4 Output
 1496: 
 1497:     HashRef -- C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'heatmap', html =E<gt> Str }>;
 1498:                embeddable fragment; no DOCTYPE, no page shell, no D3 CDN tag.
 1499: 
 1500: =cut

 1746: 
 1747: =head2 render_bar_chart_snippet
 1748: 
 1749:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars);
 1750:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars, \%opts);
 1751: 
 1752: Generates an embeddable D3.js v7 bar chart fragment.  Returns a hashref
 1753: C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'bar_chart', html =E<gt> $str }> where C<$str> is a
 1754: self-contained HTML fragment (no page shell, no D3 CDN tag) that the caller
 1755: embeds directly after loading D3.js.  C<$str> is a Perl character string
 1756: with the UTF-8 flag set (or pure ASCII when all labels are ASCII).
 1757: 
 1758: Each element of C<\@bars> is an array reference:
 1759: 
 1760:     [ $label, $value ]
 1761:     [ $label, $value, \%extra ]
 1762: 
 1763: C<$label> is the category name (string); C<$value> is a non-negative number
 1764: (negative values are silently converted to their absolute value); the optional
 1765: C<\%extra> hashref supplies additional key/value pairs shown in the hover
 1766: tooltip.  Data points with an undefined C<$value> are silently skipped.
 1767: 
 1768: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1769: 
 1770: =over 4
 1771: 
 1772: =item C<orientation> (string, default C<'vertical'>)
 1773: 
 1774: C<'vertical'> draws bars rising from a horizontal category axis.
 1775: C<'horizontal'> draws bars extending from a vertical category axis.
 1776: 
 1777: =item C<sort_bars> (string, default C<'none'>)
 1778: 
 1779: Sort order applied before rendering: C<'value'> sorts descending by bar
 1780: height, C<'label'> sorts ascending alphabetically, C<'none'> preserves the
 1781: input order.
 1782: 
 1783: =item C<max_bars> (integer, default C<0>)
 1784: 
 1785: When non-zero, only the first C<max_bars> entries (after any sort) are
 1786: rendered; the remaining entries are collapsed into a single C<Other> bar
 1787: whose value equals their sum.  Zero means no limit.
 1788: 
 1789: =item C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>)
 1790: 
 1791: Either a CSS colour value (e.g. C<'steelblue'>, C<'#4e79a7'>) applied to all
 1792: bars, or the special token C<'categorical'>, which colours each bar
 1793: distinctively using D3's Tableau-10 palette.
 1794: 
 1795: =item C<show_values> (bool, default C<0>)
 1796: 
 1797: When true, the numeric value of each bar is printed above vertical bars or
 1798: to the right of horizontal bars.
 1799: 
 1800: =item C<animated> (bool, default C<0>)
 1801: 
 1802: When true, bars grow from the baseline on page load: vertical bars rise from
 1803: the bottom; horizontal bars extend from the left.  An 800 ms D3 transition
 1804: with a per-bar stagger up to 100 ms is used.  The animation is suppressed
 1805: when C<prefers-reduced-motion> is set in the viewer's OS.
 1806: 
 1807: =item C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>)
 1808: 
 1809: Label shown in the hover tooltip before the numeric value.  The value is
 1810: JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1811: 
 1812: =item C<x_label> (string, default C<''>)
 1813: 
 1814: When non-empty, a text label is rendered below the bottom axis.  The value
 1815: is JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1816: 
 1817: =back
 1818: 
 1819: =head3 Errors
 1820: 
 1821: =over 4
 1822: 
 1823: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an
 1824: ARRAY reference.
 1825: 
 1826: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be an array reference> when an element
 1827: is not an array reference.
 1828: 
 1829: =item * Throws C<Each data point must have at least 2 elements> when an
 1830: element has fewer than 2 items.
 1831: 
 1832: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a C<$value> is not a number.
 1833: 
 1834: =item * Throws C<orientation must be 'vertical' or 'horizontal'> on an
 1835: invalid orientation value.
 1836: 
 1837: =item * Throws C<sort_bars must be 'value', 'label', or 'none'> on an
 1838: invalid sort_bars value.
 1839: 
 1840: =back
 1841: 
 1842: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1843: 
 1844: =head4 Input
 1845: 
 1846:     {
 1847:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 1848:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1 },
 1849: 	orientation => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'vertical', 'horizontal' ], optional => 1 },
 1850: 	sort_bars => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'value', 'label', 'none' ], optional => 1 }
 1851:     }
 1852: 
 1853:     Each element of C<$data>: C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 1854:     undef C<$value> silently skipped; negative C<$value> becomes positive.
 1855: 
 1856: =head4 Output
 1857: 
 1858:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'bar_chart', html => Str }
 1859:     html is a Perl character string (UTF-8 flag set, or pure ASCII).
 1860: 
 1861: =cut
 1862:

 2200: =head2 render_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2201: 
 2202:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart_with_tooltips($data);
 2203: 
 2204: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2205: Accepts the following arguments:
 2206: 
 2207: =over 4
 2208: 
 2209: =item * C<$data> - An array reference containing data points. Each data point should
 2210: be an array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
 2211: 
 2212: =back
 2213: 
 2214: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2215: The JavaScript tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> (with a backslash) rather than
 2216: C<< </b> >> to satisfy html-tidy's requirement that C<< </ >> followed by a
 2217: letter not appear literally inside C<< <script> >> blocks.
 2218: 
 2219: =head3 Errors
 2220: 
 2221: =over 4
 2222: 
 2223: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 2224: 
 2225: =back
 2226: 
 2227: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2228: 
 2229: =head4 Input
 2230: 
 2231:     {
 2232:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2233:     }
 2234: 
 2235:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
 2236:     non-arrayref dies.
 2237: 
 2238: =head4 Output
 2239: 
 2240:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; mouseover tooltip reveals label and value.
 2241:            Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> not C<< </b> >>.
 2242: 
 2243: =cut
 2244: 
 2245: sub render_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2246: {
 2247: 	my ($self, $data) = @_;
 2248: 
 2249: 	# Validate input data
 2250: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($data) eq 'ARRAY';
 2251: 
 2252: 	# Generate JSON for data
 2253: 	my $json_data = $_JSON->encode([
 2254: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
 2255: 	]);
 2256: 
 2257: 	# Generate HTML and D3.js code
 2258: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
 2259: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
 2260: <head>
 2261:     <meta charset="UTF-8">
 2262:     <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
 2263:     <title>$self->{title}</title>
 2264:     <script src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
 2265:     <style>
 2266: 	.tooltip {
 2267: 	    position: absolute;
 2268: 	    background-color: white;
 2269: 	    border: 1px solid #ccc;
 2270: 	    padding: 5px;
 2271: 	    font-size: 12px;
 2272: 	    pointer-events: none;
 2273: 	    opacity: 0;
 2274: 	    transition: opacity 0.2s ease-in-out;
 2275: 	}
 2276:     </style>
 2277: </head>
 2278: <body>
 2279:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">$self->{title}</h1>
 2280:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
 2281:     <div class="tooltip" id="tooltip"></div>
 2282:     <script>
 2283: 	const data = $json_data;
 2284: 
 2285: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
 2286: 	const tooltip = d3.select("#tooltip");
 2287: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
 2288: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
 2289: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
 2290: 
 2291: 	const x = d3.scalePoint()
 2292: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
 2293: 	    .range([0, width]);
 2294: 
 2295: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
 2296: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
 2297: 	    .nice()
 2298: 	    .range([height, 0]);
 2299: 
 2300: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
 2301: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
 2302: 
 2303: 	// Draw line
 2304: 	const line = d3.line()
 2305: 	    .x(d => x(d.label))
 2306: 	    .y(d => y(d.value));
 2307: 
 2308: 	chart.append("path")
 2309: 	    .datum(data)
 2310: 	    .attr("fill", "none")
 2311: 	    .attr("stroke", "steelblue")
 2312: 	    .attr("stroke-width", 2)
 2313: 	    .attr("d", line);
 2314: 
 2315: 	// Add points to the line
 2316: 	chart.selectAll("circle")
 2317: 	    .data(data)
 2318: 	    .join("circle")
 2319: 	    .attr("cx", d => x(d.label))
 2320: 	    .attr("cy", d => y(d.value))
 2321: 	    .attr("r", 4)
 2322: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue")
 2323: 	    .on("mouseover", (event, d) => {
 2324: 		tooltip.style("opacity", 1)
 2325: 		       .html(`Label: <b>\${d.label}<\\/b><br>Value: <b>\${d.value}<\\/b>`)
 2326: 		       .style("left", (event.pageX + 10) + "px")
 2327: 		       .style("top", (event.pageY - 30) + "px");
 2328: 	    })
 2329: 	    .on("mousemove", (event) => {
 2330: 		tooltip.style("left", (event.pageX + 10) + "px")
 2331: 		       .style("top", (event.pageY - 30) + "px");
 2332: 	    })
 2333: 	    .on("mouseout", () => {
 2334: 		tooltip.style("opacity", 0);
 2335: 	    });
 2336: 
 2337: 	// Add axes
 2338: 	chart.append("g")
 2339: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
 2340: 
 2341: 	chart.append("g")
 2342: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
 2343: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
 2344: 	    .selectAll("text")
 2345: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
 2346: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
 2347:     </script>
 2348: </body>
 2349: </html>
 2350: HTML
 2351: 
 2352:     return $html;
 2353: }

 2359:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2360:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)

 2362: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2363: Unlike C<render_line_chart_with_tooltips>, this method returns a fragment with

 2365: splicing directly into a Mojolicious TT (or any other) layout.

 2370: 
 2371: =head3 Arguments
 2372:

 2378: pairs to display in the tooltip after the label and value rows.
 2379: 
 2380:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 2381:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 2382: 
 2383: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference of rendering options:
 2384: 
 2385: =over 4
 2386: 
 2387: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - Override the C<id> attribute of
 2388: the C<<svg>> element.  Use this when embedding multiple charts on the same page.
 2389: 
 2390: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default C<0>) - If true, emit
 2391: C<viewBox="0 0 W H" width="100%" height="auto"> instead of fixed pixel
 2392: dimensions, so the chart scales with its container.  Falls back to
 2393: C<< $self->{responsive} >> when not set per call.
 2394: 
 2395: =back
 2396: 
 2397: =back

 2403: =over 4
 2404: 
 2405: =item * C<svg_id> - The C<id> attribute used on the C<<svg>> element.
 2406: 
 2407: =item * C<html> - The embeddable fragment string (Perl character string).
 2408: 
 2409: =back
 2410: 
 2411: =head3 Errors
 2412: 
 2413: =over 4
 2414: 
 2415: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an
 2416: ARRAY reference.
 2417: 
 2418: =back

 2430:             optional => 1,

 2552: =head2 render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet
 2553: 
 2554:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data);
 2555:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data, { animated => 1 });
 2556:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2557:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + button + svg + script)
 2558: 
 2559: Like C<render_line_chart_snippet>, but adds brush-to-zoom: the user can drag
 2560: across a range of the x-axis to zoom into that region. A I<Reset zoom> button
 2561: (hidden until a zoom is active) returns the chart to its original extent.
 2562: Subsequent brushes on the zoomed view zoom in further; Reset always returns to
 2563: the full dataset.
 2564: 
 2565: The caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page C<<head>>.
 2566: 
 2567: Accepts the same arguments as C<render_line_chart_snippet>: an array reference
 2568: of data points, each C<[$x, $y]> or C<[$x, $y, \%extra]>, plus an optional
 2569: second argument C<$opts> (hashref).
 2570: 
 2571: =head3 Options
 2572: 
 2573: =over 4
 2574: 
 2575: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>) - when true, the initial page load
 2576: animates the line drawing left-to-right via the C<stroke-dashoffset> technique
 2577: (1200 ms, C<d3.easeLinear>), then fades in data-point circles after the line
 2578: finishes (300 ms after a 1200 ms delay).  Respects
 2579: C<prefers-reduced-motion>: when the user has requested reduced motion the line
 2580: is drawn immediately at full opacity.  Subsequent zoom and reset redraws are
 2581: never animated regardless of this flag.
 2582: 
 2583: =back
 2584: 
 2585: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2586: 
 2587: =head4 Input
 2588: 
 2589:     {

 2842: 
 2843: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a chart of many lines with mouseover tooltips.

 2846: 
 2847: =over 4
 2848: 
 2849: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of series hashes. Each element is a hashref
 2850: with a C<name> key (string) and a C<data> key (array reference of C<< {label, value} >>
 2851: hashrefs).
 2852: 
 2853:     [
 2854:         { name => 'Series A', data => [{ label => 'Jan', value => 100 }, ...] },
 2855:         ...
 2856:     ]
 2857: 
 2858: =back
 2859: 
 2860: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2861: Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> rather than C<< </b> >> for html-tidy compliance.

 2878:

 2880:     C<data> (arrayref of hashrefs with C<label> and C<value> keys);

 3583: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet

  Mutants (Total: 2, Killed: 2, Survived: 0)
 3584: 
 3585:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series);
 3586:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series, \%opts);
 3587:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 3588:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 3589: 
 3590: Generates an embeddable multi-series line chart fragment.  Unlike
 3591: C<render_multi_series_line_chart_with_interactive_legends>, this method returns
 3592: a bare fragment with no C<< <html> >>, C<< <head> >>, or C<< <body> >> wrapper;
 3593: D3.js must be loaded by the host page.
 3594: 
 3595: =head3 Arguments
 3596: 
 3597: =over 4
 3598: 
 3599: =item * C<\@series> - An array reference of series.  Each series is a hash
 3600: reference with a C<name> (string) and a C<data> array reference of
 3601: C<< { label => Str, value => Num } >> points:
 3602: 
 3603:     [
 3604:         { name => 'North', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value => 12 }, ... ] },
 3605:         { name => 'South', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value =>  8 }, ... ] },
 3606:     ]
 3607: 
 3608: Series need not share labels: the X domain is the union of every series' labels,
 3609: in first-seen order.  One line and one set of dots is drawn per series, and a
 3610: clickable legend toggles each series on and off.
 3611: 
 3612: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 3613: 
 3614: =over 4
 3615: 
 3616: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 3617: scheme (for example C<'category10'>, C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, C<'paired'>).
 3618: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 3619: the colours of one.
 3620: 
 3621: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown below the X axis.
 3622: 
 3623: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown beside the Y axis.
 3624: 
 3625: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true each line draws itself left
 3626: to right by animating its stroke dash offset.  Respects
 3627: C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 3628: 
 3629: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 3630: 
 3631: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 3632: 
 3633: =back
 3634: 
 3635: =back
 3636: 
 3637: Returns C<< { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str } >>.
 3638: 
 3639: =head3 Errors
 3640: 
 3641: =over 4
 3642: 
 3643: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of series> when C<\@series> is not an
 3644: ARRAY reference.
 3645: 
 3646: =item * Throws C<Each series must be a hashref with name and data> when a
 3647: series is not a hashref, has no C<name>, or has a C<data> that is not an ARRAY
 3648: reference.
 3649: 
 3650: =back
 3651: 
 3652: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 3653: 
 3654: =head4 Input
 3655: 
 3656:     {
 3657:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 3658:         opts => {
 3659:             type     => 'hashref',
 3660:             optional => 1,
 3661:             keys     => {
 3662:                 scheme     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 3663:                 x_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3664:                 y_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3665:                 animated   => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3666:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 3667:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'chart' },
 3668:             },
 3669:         },
 3670:     }
 3671: 
 3672: =head4 Output
 3673: 
 3674:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str }
 3675: 
 3676: =cut

 3859: 
 3860: =head2 render_scatter_chart_snippet
 3861: 
 3862:     my $fragment = $chart->render_scatter_chart_snippet($data);
 3863:     my $fragment = $chart->render_scatter_chart_snippet($data, \%opts);
 3864:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 3865:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 3866: 
 3867: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a scatter plot with mouseover
 3868: tooltips.  Returns a fragment with no C<<!DOCTYPE>>, C<<html>>, C<<head>>, or
 3869: C<<body>> wrapper; the caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page.
 3870: 
 3871: =head3 Arguments
 3872: 
 3873: =over 4
 3874: 
 3875: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each point is an array
 3876: reference with two required numeric elements (x, y), an optional third hash
 3877: reference of extra key/value pairs shown as extra tooltip rows, and an optional
 3878: fourth hash reference carrying the C<series> (colour group) and C<size> (bubble
 3879: radius) dimensions:
 3880: 
 3881:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 3882:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 3883:     [$x, $y, \%row, { series => 'North', size => 42 }]   # colour and size
 3884: 
 3885: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 3886: 
 3887: =over 4
 3888: 
 3889: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'scatter_chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 3890: 
 3891: =item * C<color> (CSS colour or C<'categorical'>, default C<'steelblue'>) -
 3892: Fill colour for the data points.  C<'categorical'> uses the Tableau-10 palette.
 3893: 
 3894: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the X axis.
 3895: 
 3896: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the Y axis.
 3897: 
 3898: =item * C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Prefix for the tooltip value row.
 3899: 
 3900: =item * C<colour_by_series> (boolean, default 0) - Colour each point by its
 3901: C<series> value instead of by point index, and draw a legend listing the series
 3902: to the right of the plot.  Ignored for points with no C<series> value.
 3903: 
 3904: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 3905: scheme used when C<colour_by_series> is set, for example C<'category10'>,
 3906: C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, or C<'paired'>.
 3907: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 3908: the colours of one.
 3909: 
 3910: =item * C<series_col> (string, default C<'Series'>) - Row label for the series
 3911: value in the tooltip.
 3912: 
 3913: =item * C<size_col> (string, default C<'Size'>) - Row label for the size value
 3914: in the tooltip.
 3915: 
 3916: =item * C<radius_range> (arrayref of two numbers, default C<[3, 15]>) - Pixel
 3917: radii mapped from the smallest and largest observed C<size> value.  The mapping
 3918: is by area (C<d3.scaleSqrt>), so a point twice as large is drawn with four
 3919: times the area.  A point with no C<size> value is drawn at the lower radius.
 3920: 
 3921: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true, circles fade in from
 3922: opacity 0 on page load (400 ms).  Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 3923: 
 3924: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default 0) - See C<render_bar_chart_snippet>.
 3925: 
 3926: =back
 3927: 
 3928: =back
 3929: 
 3930: =head3 Return value
 3931: 
 3932: A hash reference with C<svg_id> (string) and C<html> (Perl character string).
 3933: 
 3934: =head3 Errors
 3935: 
 3936: =over 4
 3937: 
 3938: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY ref.
 3939: 
 3940: =item * Dies with C<'Each data point must be an array reference'> when an element is not an ARRAY ref.
 3941: 
 3942: =item * Dies with C<'Each data point must have at least 2 elements'> when a point has fewer than 2 items.
 3943: 
 3944: =item * Dies with C<'X value must be numeric'> when the x element is not a number.
 3945: 
 3946: =item * Dies with C<'Y value must be numeric'> when the y element is not a number.
 3947: 
 3948: =back
 3949: 
 3950: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 3951: 
 3952:     {
 3953:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 3954:         opts => {
 3955:             type     => 'hashref',
 3956:             optional => 1,
 3957:             keys     => {
 3958:                 id          => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 3959:                 color       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 3960:                 x_label     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3961:                 y_label     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3962:                 value_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'Value' },
 3963:                 colour_by_series => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3964:                 scheme      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 3965:                 series_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3966:                 size_col    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3967:                 radius_range => { type => 'arrayref', optional => 1, default => [3, 15] },
 3968:                 animated    => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3969:                 responsive  => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3970:             },
 3971:         },
 3972:     }
 3973: 
 3974: =cut

 4247: =head2 render_table_snippet
 4248: 
 4249:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data);
 4250:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data, \%opts);
 4251:     # $fragment->{table_id} - the id attribute of the <table> element
 4252:     # $fragment->{html}     - embeddable HTML fragment (style + table + script)
 4253: 
 4254: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a sortable, filterable data table.
 4255: Returns a fragment with no page-shell wrapper; the caller loads D3 if needed.
 4256: 
 4257: =head3 Arguments
 4258: 
 4259: =over 4
 4260: 
 4261: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of row array references.  The first row
 4262: is treated as the header row.  Every subsequent row must have the same number
 4263: of columns as the header.
 4264: 
 4265:     [ ['Name', 'Value', 'Category'],   # header
 4266:       ['Alpha',   300,  'A'],
 4267:       ['Beta',    150,  'B'],
 4268:     ]
 4269: 
 4270: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 4271: 
 4272: =over 4
 4273: 
 4274: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'data_table'>) - C<id> of the C<<table>> element.
 4275: 
 4276: =item * C<sortable> (boolean, default 1) - If true, clicking a column header
 4277: sorts the table by that column (toggle ascending/descending).
 4278: 
 4279: =item * C<caption> (string, default C<''>) - Optional C<<caption>> element text.
 4280: 
 4281: =back
 4282: 
 4283: =back
 4284: 
 4285: =head3 Return value
 4286: 
 4287: A hash reference with C<table_id> (string) and C<html> (Perl character string).
 4288: Note: returns C<table_id>, not C<svg_id>, because this method renders an HTML
 4289: table rather than an SVG chart.
 4290: 
 4291: =head3 Errors
 4292: 
 4293: =over 4
 4294: 
 4295: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY ref.
 4296: 
 4297: =item * Dies with C<'Data must have at least one row (header row)'> when C<$data> is empty.
 4298: 
 4299: =item * Dies with C<'Each row must be an array reference'> when a row is not an ARRAY ref.
 4300: 
 4301: =back
 4302: 
 4303: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4304: 
 4305:     {
 4306:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4307:         opts => {
 4308:             type     => 'hashref',
 4309:             optional => 1,
 4310:             keys     => {
 4311:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'data_table' },
 4312:                 sortable => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 4313:                 caption  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 4314:             },
 4315:         },
 4316:     }
 4317: 
 4318: =cut
 4319:

 4442: 
 4443: =head2 render_stacked_bar_snippet
 4444: 
 4445:     my $result = $chart->render_stacked_bar_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4446: 
 4447: Generates an embeddable stacked bar chart (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 4448: 
 4449: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with three keys named by C<opts>:
 4450: C<cat_col> (the X-axis grouping), C<seg_col> (the stacking segment), and
 4451: C<val_col> (the numeric measure).  Multiple rows sharing the same
 4452: cat+seg combination are summed.
 4453: 
 4454: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4455: 
 4456: =over 4
 4457: 
 4458: =item * C<cat_col> (string, default C<'cat'>) - row key for the X-axis category.
 4459: 
 4460: =item * C<seg_col> (string, default C<'seg'>) - row key for the stacking segment.
 4461: 
 4462: =item * C<val_col> (string, default C<'val'>) - row key for the numeric value.
 4463: 
 4464: =item * C<orient> (string C<'v'> or C<'h'>, default C<'v'>) - bar orientation.
 4465: 
 4466: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 4467: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 4468: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 4469: Perl.
 4470: 
 4471: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - axis label shown below the category axis.
 4472: 
 4473: =item * C<sort> (string, optional) - sort categories: C<'value'> (desc total)
 4474: or C<'label'> (alpha).
 4475: 
 4476: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4477: 
 4478: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4479: 
 4480: =back
 4481: 
 4482: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'stacked_bar', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4483: 
 4484: =head3 Errors
 4485: 
 4486: =over 4
 4487: 
 4488: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4489: 
 4490: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4491: 
 4492: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a value field is not a number.
 4493: 
 4494: =item * Throws C<orient must be 'v' or 'h'> for an invalid C<orient> value.
 4495: 
 4496: =item * Throws C<sort must be 'value' or 'label'> for an invalid C<sort> value.
 4497: 
 4498: =back
 4499: 
 4500: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4501: 
 4502: =head4 Input
 4503: 
 4504:     {
 4505:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4506:         opts => {
 4507:             type     => 'hashref',
 4508:             optional => 1,
 4509:             keys     => {
 4510:                 cat_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'cat' },
 4511:                 seg_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'seg' },
 4512:                 val_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'val' },
 4513:                 orient   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'v' },
 4514:                 scheme   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 4515:                 x_label  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 4516:                 sort     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 4517:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'stacked_bar' },
 4518:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 4519:             },
 4520:         },
 4521:     }
 4522: 
 4523: =head4 Output
 4524: 
 4525:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 4526: 
 4527: =cut

 4735: 
 4736: =head2 render_combo_snippet
 4737: 
 4738:     my $result = $chart->render_combo_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4739: 
 4740: Generates an embeddable combo chart: bars for one measure on the left Y
 4741: axis, a line for another measure on the right Y axis, sharing the same
 4742: X (category) axis.
 4743: 
 4744: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with at least three keys named by
 4745: C<opts>: C<x_col> (X-axis label), C<bar_col> (bar measure), and
 4746: C<line_col> (line measure).
 4747: 
 4748: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4749: 
 4750: =over 4
 4751: 
 4752: =item * C<x_col> (string, default C<'x'>) - row key for the X-axis label.
 4753: 
 4754: =item * C<bar_col> (string, default C<'bar_val'>) - row key for the bar measure.
 4755: 
 4756: =item * C<line_col> (string, default C<'line_val'>) - row key for the line measure.
 4757: 
 4758: =item * C<bar_label> (string, default C<'Bar'>) - legend/axis label for bars.
 4759: 
 4760: =item * C<line_label> (string, default C<'Line'>) - legend/axis label for line.
 4761: 
 4762: =item * C<bar_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill.
 4763: 
 4764: =item * C<line_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'#e15759'>) - line stroke.
 4765: 
 4766: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4767: 
 4768: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4769: 
 4770: =back
 4771: 
 4772: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'combo_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4773: 
 4774: =head3 Errors
 4775: 
 4776: =over 4
 4777: 
 4778: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4779: 
 4780: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4781: 
 4782: =item * Throws C<Bar value must be numeric> when a bar value field is not a number.
 4783: 
 4784: =item * Throws C<Line value must be numeric> when a line value field is not a number.
 4785: 
 4786: =back
 4787: 
 4788: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4789: 
 4790: =head4 Input
 4791: 
 4792:     {
 4793:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4794:         opts => {
 4795:             type     => 'hashref',
 4796:             optional => 1,
 4797:             keys     => {
 4798:                 x_col      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'x' },
 4799:                 bar_col    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'bar_val' },
 4800:                 line_col   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'line_val' },
 4801:                 bar_label  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'Bar' },
 4802:                 line_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'Line' },
 4803:                 bar_color  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 4804:                 line_color => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '#e15759' },
 4805:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'combo_chart' },
 4806:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 4807:             },
 4808:         },
 4809:     }
 4810: 
 4811: =head4 Output
 4812: 
 4813:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 4814: 
 4815: =cut

 4986: =head2 render_treemap_snippet
 4987: 
 4988:     my $result = $chart->render_treemap_snippet(\@rows_or_tree, \%opts);
 4989: 
 4990: Generates an embeddable treemap (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 4991: Leaf area is proportional to the numeric measure.
 4992: 
 4993: Accepts two data formats:
 4994: 
 4995: =over 4
 4996: 
 4997: =item * B<Flat path> (arrayref of hashrefs): each row must have a C<path>
 4998: key (e.g. C<'Root/A/X'>) and a C<value> key.  The module converts this
 4999: to a D3 stratify hierarchy in JS.  Override the path separator with
 5000: C<opts-E<gt>{path_sep}>.
 5001: 
 5002: =item * B<Nested tree> (hashref): a pre-built D3-compatible hierarchy
 5003: with C<name>/C<children>/C<value> structure.  Passed directly to
 5004: C<d3.hierarchy()>.
 5005: 
 5006: =back
 5007: 
 5008: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5009: 
 5010: =over 4
 5011: 
 5012: =item * C<path_sep> (string, default C<'/'>)  - path separator (flat format only).
 5013: 
 5014: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 5015: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 5016: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 5017: Perl.
 5018: 
 5019: =item * C<label> (boolean, default C<1>) - show node labels inside cells.
 5020: 
 5021: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5022: 
 5023: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5024: 
 5025: =back
 5026: 
 5027: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'treemap', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5028: 
 5029: =head3 Errors
 5030: 
 5031: =over 4
 5032: 
 5033: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes or a hash tree>
 5034: when C<$data> is neither an ARRAY nor a HASH reference.
 5035: 
 5036: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must be a hash reference> when a flat row is not a hashref.
 5037: 
 5038: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a 'path' key> when a flat row lacks C<path>.
 5039: 
 5040: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a numeric 'value' key> when a value is not numeric.
 5041: 
 5042: =back
 5043: 
 5044: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5045: 
 5046: =head4 Input
 5047: 
 5048:     {
 5049:         data => { type => 'arrayref | hashref' },
 5050:         opts => {
 5051:             type     => 'hashref',
 5052:             optional => 1,
 5053:             keys     => {
 5054:                 path_sep => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '/' },
 5055:                 scheme   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 5056:                 label    => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 5057:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'treemap' },
 5058:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5059:             },
 5060:         },
 5061:     }
 5062: 
 5063: =head4 Output
 5064: 
 5065:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5066: 
 5067: =cut

 5214: 
 5215: =head2 render_histogram_snippet
 5216: 
 5217:     my $result = $chart->render_histogram_snippet(\@values, \%opts);
 5218: 
 5219: Generates an embeddable frequency histogram (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 5220: C<\@values> is an arrayref of numeric scalars (already extracted and
 5221: coerced by the caller).
 5222: 
 5223: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5224: 
 5225: =over 4
 5226: 
 5227: =item * C<bins> (integer or C<undef>, default C<20>) - number of bins.
 5228: Pass C<undef> for automatic bin count using Sturges' rule:
 5229: C<ceil(log2(n) + 1)>.  Ignored when C<binned> is set.
 5230: 
 5231: =item * C<binned> (boolean, default 0) - treat C<$data> as pre-computed bins
 5232: rather than as raw values.  Each element is a hashref with a C<count> and
 5233: either a C<label> or a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair from which the label C<[lo,hi)>
 5234: is derived:
 5235: 
 5236:     { label => '[0,10)', count => 3 }
 5237:     { lo => 10, hi => 20, count => 5 }
 5238: 
 5239: The caller owns the binning, so C<d3.bin()> is never called: one bar is drawn
 5240: per entry, in the order supplied, and the supplied counts are authoritative.
 5241: 
 5242: =item * C<scheme> (string, optional) - D3 categorical colour scheme (for example
 5243: C<'tableau10'> or C<'category10'>) whose first colour is used as the bar fill
 5244: when C<color> is not given.
 5245: That colour is C<colour_at($scheme, 0)>, so a caller shading the same data by
 5246: hand starts from the same colour the bars do.
 5247: 
 5248: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - label shown below the X axis.
 5249: 
 5250: =item * C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill colour.
 5251: 
 5252: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5253: 
 5254: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5255: 
 5256: =back
 5257: 
 5258: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'histogram', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5259: 
 5260: =head3 Errors
 5261: 
 5262: =over 4
 5263: 
 5264: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of numeric values> when C<$data> is
 5265: not an ARRAY reference.
 5266: 
 5267: =item * Throws C<Each value must be numeric> when any element is non-numeric.
 5268: 
 5269: =item * Throws C<bins must be a positive integer> when C<bins> is defined
 5270: but not a positive integer.
 5271: 
 5272: =item * Throws C<Data must contain at least one bin> when C<binned> is set and
 5273: C<$data> holds no bins.
 5274: 
 5275: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must be a hash reference> when a
 5276: pre-binned element is not a hashref.
 5277: 
 5278: =item * Throws C<Bin count must be numeric> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5279: missing or not numeric.
 5280: 
 5281: =item * Throws C<Bin count must not be negative> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5282: negative.
 5283: 
 5284: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must have a label or numeric lo and hi>
 5285: when a pre-binned entry has neither a C<label> nor a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair.
 5286: 
 5287: =back
 5288: 
 5289: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5290: 
 5291: =head4 Input
 5292: 
 5293:     {
 5294:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 5295:         opts => {
 5296:             type     => 'hashref',
 5297:             optional => 1,
 5298:             keys     => {
 5299:                 bins    => { type => 'integer', optional => 1, default => 20 },
 5300:                 binned  => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 5301:                 x_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 5302:                 color   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 5303:                 scheme  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 5304:                 id      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'histogram' },
 5305:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5306:             },
 5307:         },
 5308:     }
 5309: 
 5310: =head4 Output
 5311: 
 5312:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5313: 
 5314: =cut
 5315:
